# ./commerce/server/postgres API declaration snapshot

Maturity: **experimental**.

Development-preview API; no stable compatibility claim.

Generated by `node --experimental-strip-types ./scripts/api-contract.ts --update`. Every change requires explicit review.

## dist/bff/dispatch.d.ts

```ts
import type { HttpRequest, HttpResponse } from "../http/index.js";
export type RouteEntry = {
    route: string;
    pattern: RegExp;
    params: string[];
    handler: (req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse) => unknown;
    /** Fail unsupported hosted bundles closed before auth without weakening direct-route auth. */
    availability?: "direct-postgres";
};
/** Match `routes` against one request and run the handler, or answer not found. */
export type BffDispatch = (routes: readonly RouteEntry[], req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse) => Promise<unknown>;
export interface BffDispatcherOptions {
    /** Environment read on each request to decide a route's bundle availability. */
    env: () => Record<string, string | undefined>;
    /** Writes the deployment's not-found response for an unmatched or unavailable path. */
    notFound: (res: HttpResponse, pathname: string) => void;
    /** Runs once per request before matching (for example, ambient request configuration). */
    beforeDispatch?: () => void;
    /**
     * Runs after matching and before the handler, for example route authorization; resolving false
     * means it already answered. Required, so no composition skips it by omission; a deployment
     * without guarded routes passes `() => true`.
     */
    preflight: (req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse, route: string) => Promise<boolean> | boolean;
}
/** Build the dispatcher a deployment mounts on its hosted and Node entrypoints alike. */
export declare function createBffDispatcher(options: BffDispatcherOptions): BffDispatch;
```

```ts
import type { HttpResponse } from "../http/index.js";
export declare const BFF_REQUEST_REFERENCE_HEADER = "x-request-id";
export declare const BFF_ERROR_CODES: readonly ["BAD_REQUEST", "UNAUTHORIZED", "FORBIDDEN", "NOT_FOUND", "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED", "CONFLICT", "RATE_LIMITED", "UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE", "INTERNAL", "INVALID_RESPONSE"];
export type BffErrorCode = (typeof BFF_ERROR_CODES)[number];
export type BffMeta = {
    requestId?: string;
    contractVersion?: string;
    [key: string]: unknown;
};
export type BffError = {
    code: BffErrorCode;
    message: string;
    details?: unknown;
};
export type BffSuccessResponse<T> = {
    ok: true;
    data: T;
    meta?: BffMeta;
};
export type BffErrorResponse = {
    ok: false;
    error: BffError;
    meta?: BffMeta;
};
export type BffResponse<T> = BffSuccessResponse<T> | BffErrorResponse;
/** The HTTP status each error code answers with unless the caller names another. */
export declare const BFF_ERROR_STATUS: Readonly<Record<BffErrorCode, number>>;
export interface BffErrorOptions {
    status?: number;
    details?: unknown;
    meta?: BffMeta;
}
export interface BffSuccessOptions {
    cacheControl?: string;
}
export interface BffResponderOptions {
    /** The request reference observed for this response, if the deployment records one. */
    responseRequestId?: (res: HttpResponse) => string | undefined;
}
export interface BffResponder {
    sendBffSuccess<T>(res: HttpResponse, data: T, meta?: BffMeta, options?: BffSuccessOptions): void;
    sendBffError(res: HttpResponse, code: BffErrorCode, message: string, options?: BffErrorOptions): void;
    sendMethodNotAllowed(res: HttpResponse, allowedMethods: readonly string[]): void;
}
/** Build the envelope writers a deployment's handlers share. */
export declare function createBffResponder(options?: BffResponderOptions): BffResponder;
```

```ts
export interface HealthContract<T extends {
    contractVersion: string;
}> {
    safeParse(value: unknown): {
        success: true;
        data: T;
    } | {
        success: false;
    };
}
export interface HealthHandlerOptions<T extends {
    contractVersion: string;
}> {
    responder: BffResponder;
    /** The deployment's strict health contract. */
    contract: HealthContract<T>;
    /** Builds this request's health payload: service identity, release provenance and the rest. */
    read: () => unknown;
}
export declare function createHealthHandler<T extends {
    contractVersion: string;
}>(options: HealthHandlerOptions<T>): (req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse) => void;
```

```ts
import type { HttpRequest, HttpResponse } from "../http/index.js";
export interface ObservedRequestContext {
    readonly requestId: string;
}
export interface RequestContextOptions {
    /** Chooses this request's reference. Called once per request. */
    readRequestId: (req: HttpRequest) => string;
}
export interface RequestContextStore {
    /** Create (or reuse) the request's context and attach it to the response. */
    install(req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse): ObservedRequestContext;
    /** The request's context, created on first use. */
    readOrCreate(req: HttpRequest): ObservedRequestContext;
    /** The context attached to a response by `install`, if any. */
    readResponse(res: HttpResponse): ObservedRequestContext | undefined;
}
export declare function createRequestContextStore(options: RequestContextOptions): RequestContextStore;
```

```ts
        scrap: "scrap";

}, z.core.$strip>;
/** Bind host reason codes for request/approve/reject only; restock/refund fields record intent, without execution. */

    returnRequestCreateSchema: z.ZodObject<{
        idempotencyKey: z.ZodString;
        orderId: z.ZodGUID;

                scrap: "scrap";
            }>>;
        }, z.core.$strip>>;
        customerNote: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
    }, z.core.$strip>;
};

            quarantine: "quarantine";

        }>>;
    }, z.core.$strip>>;
    customerNote: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
}, z.core.$strip>;
/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */

/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */

}, z.core.$strip>;
/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */
export type ReturnRequestCreateInput<Reason extends string = z.infer<typeof returnRequestCreateSchema>["reasonCode"]> = Omit<z.infer<typeof returnRequestCreateSchema>, "reasonCode"> & {
    reasonCode: Reason;
    requestedBy?: string | null;
};
/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */

};
/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */
export interface ReturnLifecycleResult {
    returnRequestId: string;
    status: string;
    replayed: boolean;
}
/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */
export interface CommerceReturnsPort<Reason extends string = ReturnRequestCreateInput["reasonCode"]> {
    createRequest(input: ReturnRequestCreateInput<Reason>): Promise<ReturnLifecycleResult>;
    approve(input: ReturnApproveInput): Promise<ReturnLifecycleResult>;
    reject(input: ReturnRejectInput): Promise<ReturnLifecycleResult>;
}
```

```ts
export type OrderMoneyIssueCode = "allocated_discount_mismatch" | "catalog_subtotal_mismatch" | "effective_subtotal_mismatch" | "header_equation_mismatch" | "item_catalog_equation_mismatch" | "item_catalog_money_out_of_range" | "item_discount_out_of_range" | "item_effective_money_out_of_range" | "item_gross_equation_mismatch" | "item_net_vat_mismatch" | "item_quantity_out_of_range" | "item_vat_rate_out_of_range" | "missing_item_canonical_money" | "mixed_vat_rates" | "product_discount_out_of_range" | "shipping_discount_out_of_range" | "tax_total_mismatch" | "total_positions_mismatch";
export interface OrderMoneyHeaderRow {
    id?: string;
    currency: string;
    subtotal_cents: number;
    discount_cents: number;
    shipping_cents: number;
    shipping_discount_cents: number;
    tax_cents: number;
    total_cents: number;
}
export interface OrderMoneyItemRow {
    id?: string;
    quantity: number;
    unit_price_cents: number;
    total_cents: number;
    discount_allocated_cents: number | null | undefined;
    effective_total_cents: number | null | undefined;
    effective_net_cents: number | null | undefined;
    vat_rate_bps: number;
}
export interface CanonicalOrderMoneyLine {
    id: string | null;
    quantity: number;
    catalogUnit: number;
    catalogTotal: number;
    discountAllocated: number;
    effectiveGross: number;
    effectiveNet: number;
    vatRateBps: number;
}
export interface CanonicalOrderMoney {
    orderId: string | null;
    currency: string;
    subtotal: number;
    productDiscount: number;
    shippingGross: number;
    shippingDiscount: number;
    shippingEffective: number;
    tax: number;
    total: number;
    lines: CanonicalOrderMoneyLine[];
    validationScope: "full" | "header";
    reconciled: boolean;
    issueCodes: OrderMoneyIssueCode[];
}
export interface OrderMoneyDiagnostic {
    event: "commerce_order_money_unreconciled";
    orderId: string | null;
    validationScope: CanonicalOrderMoney["validationScope"];
    issueCodes: OrderMoneyIssueCode[];
}
```

```ts
export declare const CART_STATUSES: readonly ["draft"];
export declare const CHECKOUT_STATUSES: readonly ["not_configured", "created", "expired"];
export declare const ORDER_DRAFT_STATUSES: readonly ["draft"];
export declare const ORDER_STATUSES: readonly ["draft", "pending_payment", "paid", "failed", "expired", "fulfillment_pending", "cancelled", "fulfilled", "refunded"];
export declare const PAYMENT_STATUSES: readonly ["not_started", "requires_action", "processing", "paid", "failed", "expired", "refunded", "partially_refunded", "disputed"];
export declare const OMS_FULFILLMENT_BLOCK_REASONS: readonly ["order_not_paid", "payment_not_succeeded", "subscription_cycle_not_paid", "active_hold", "missing_shipping_address", "inventory_review", "risk_review"];
export declare const OMS_INVENTORY_STATUSES: readonly ["not_checked", "reserved", "missing", "released", "expired", "consumed", "review_required"];
export type CartStatus = (typeof CART_STATUSES)[number];
export type CheckoutStatus = (typeof CHECKOUT_STATUSES)[number];
export type OrderDraftStatus = (typeof ORDER_DRAFT_STATUSES)[number];
export type OrderStatus = (typeof ORDER_STATUSES)[number];
export type PaymentStatus = (typeof PAYMENT_STATUSES)[number];
export type OmsFulfillmentBlockReason = (typeof OMS_FULFILLMENT_BLOCK_REASONS)[number];
export type OmsInventoryStatus = (typeof OMS_INVENTORY_STATUSES)[number];
export interface CommerceFulfillmentEligibilityInput<PaymentState extends string = string, SubscriptionState extends string = string> {
    orderMode: "one_time" | "subscription_cycle";
    orderStatus: OrderStatus;
    paymentStatus: PaymentState;
    subscriptionCycleStatus?: SubscriptionState | null;
    activeHoldCount: number;
    hasShippingAddress: boolean;
    inventoryStatus?: OmsInventoryStatus;
    inventoryReviewRequired?: boolean;
    riskReviewRequired?: boolean;
}
export type OmsPaymentStatus<PaymentState extends string> = "not_started" | PaymentState;
```

```ts
/**
 * The order's replacement chain, detail-only.
 *
 * ⛔ This is NOT a second fulfilment summary. `fulfillment` stays exactly what W3 collapsed it
 * to - one row, the parcel currently representing the order, with every companion narrowed to
 * that parcel's id. Widening those companions was measured and rejected: the dispatch refs,
 * provider attempts and status evidence of a superseded parcel would then be read by the
 * fulfilment health classifier, the fulfilment debug panel and the customer-step derivation,
 * each of which would answer for the wrong parcel - a dispatch status of `created` for a
 * replacement that has never been dispatched, or a customer step of `delivered` for a parcel
 * that is still `created`.

 * needs to answer "where did the first one go?" and to file a carrier claim. Tracking number,
 * carrier, service, the two handover instants, and the parcel's own status.
 */
```

## dist/commerce/oms/omsPorts.d.ts

```ts
import type { OmsReplacementRefusal } from "../contracts/omsTypes.js";
import { type OmsCurrencyPolicy } from "../model/omsCurrency.js";
export type { CommerceOmsHoldPort, CommerceOmsReadPort, OmsReplacementRefusal } from "../contracts/omsTypes.js";
/**
 * Every refusal `commerce_oms_request_replacement_shipment` can reach an operator with,
 * as one closed vocabulary shared by the adapter that classifies it and the admin surface
 * that renders a sentence for it. Two lists would drift, and the failure mode of drift is
 * the operator reading advice about a hold when the warehouse is simply out of stock.
 *
 * The first group is the command's own `commerce_oms_replacement_*` names. The second is
 * the reservation vocabulary raised THROUGH it: the command mints a fresh generation via
 * `inventory_reserve_order`, and on a stock authority outside this system that call is the
 * most likely legitimate refusal there is. Those errors never carried the command's prefix,
 * so before this list they matched nothing, degraded to an upstream failure, and the
 * operator was told to check holds that were not there.
 *
 * It stays an allowlist, not a translation table: a reason absent from it degrades to
 * `unspecified_refusal`, so no database text can travel to a response, a log or a screen.
 *
 * ⛔ The exported names deliberately omit the `CommerceOms` prefix every other symbol in
 * this file carries. `src/lib/commerceOmsBoundary.test.ts` forbids that token in production
 * UI files outside a small allowlist, and the admin surface imports these three symbols.
 * Renaming them is the honest way to keep that boundary guard intact; widening its allowlist
 * to admit one more file would weaken the control instead of respecting it.
 */
export declare const OMS_REPLACEMENT_COMMAND_REFUSALS: readonly ["invalid_input", "idempotency_conflict", "order_not_found", "order_not_replaceable", "missing_client", "missing_shipping_address", "shipping_address_not_found", "payment_not_succeeded", "subscription_cycle_not_paid", "no_parcel_to_replace", "undispatched_replacement_exists", "missing_order_items", "missing_catalog_sku", "missing_inventory_reservation", "blocked_by_payment_not_succeeded_hold", "blocked_by_inventory_review_hold", "blocked_by_risk_review_hold", "blocked_by_address_review_hold", "blocked_by_manual_support_hold"];
export declare const OMS_REPLACEMENT_RESERVATION_REFUSALS: readonly ["inventory_external_provider_insufficient_available_stock", "inventory_external_provider_stock_stale", "inventory_external_provider_stock_missing", "inventory_external_provider_sku_not_found", "inventory_external_provider_location_missing", "inventory_reservation_insufficient_available_stock", "inventory_reservation_allocation_incomplete", "inventory_reservation_payment_status_not_reservable", "inventory_reservation_invalid_quantity"];
export declare const UNSPECIFIED_REPLACEMENT_REFUSAL = "unspecified_refusal";
export declare const OMS_REPLACEMENT_REFUSALS: readonly ["invalid_input", "idempotency_conflict", "order_not_found", "order_not_replaceable", "missing_client", "missing_shipping_address", "shipping_address_not_found", "payment_not_succeeded", "subscription_cycle_not_paid", "no_parcel_to_replace", "undispatched_replacement_exists", "missing_order_items", "missing_catalog_sku", "missing_inventory_reservation", "blocked_by_payment_not_succeeded_hold", "blocked_by_inventory_review_hold", "blocked_by_risk_review_hold", "blocked_by_address_review_hold", "blocked_by_manual_support_hold", "inventory_external_provider_insufficient_available_stock", "inventory_external_provider_stock_stale", "inventory_external_provider_stock_missing", "inventory_external_provider_sku_not_found", "inventory_external_provider_location_missing", "inventory_reservation_insufficient_available_stock", "inventory_reservation_allocation_incomplete", "inventory_reservation_payment_status_not_reservable", "inventory_reservation_invalid_quantity", "unspecified_refusal"];
/**
 * Reads the refusal a replacement error carries, from any of the shapes a BFF error can
 * legitimately arrive in. Returns null when the text names no refusal this vocabulary
 * knows, which is the honest answer: the operator gets the generic sentence plus the raw
 * detail to hand to the team, never a guess dressed as advice.
 */
export declare function readOmsReplacementRefusal(text: string): OmsReplacementRefusal | null;
export declare class CommerceOmsInvalidResponseError extends Error {
    constructor();
}
export declare class CommerceOmsPersistenceError extends Error {
    readonly details: Record<string, unknown>;
    constructor(message?: string, details?: Record<string, unknown>);
}
/**
 * An order row came back from the database in a currency this deployment does
 * not accept. The channel ingest rail is deliberately multi-currency, so such a
 * row can genuinely exist; what it cannot do is reach the read model, whose
 * money nodes are pinned to the platform's accepted set.
 *
 * A subclass rather than a new error family, so it inherits the persistence
 * error's HTTP mapping untouched: this is still an upstream read that could not
 * be served, and the operator-facing status must not move. The gain is entirely
 * diagnostic - the failure now names the orders and the codes instead of
 * surfacing as an anonymous validation error fifteen frames later.
 */
export declare class CommerceOmsUnsupportedCurrencyError extends CommerceOmsPersistenceError {
    readonly orderIds: readonly string[];
    readonly currencies: readonly string[];
    constructor(offenders: ReadonlyArray<{
        orderId: string;
        currency: string;
    }>);
}
/**
 * Guards the one field of an order row that can break the OMS read.
 *
 * This is deliberately not a schema for the whole row. Of that row's ~22
 * columns exactly one - `currency` - is both able to fail the read model and
 * something the platform holds an opinion about; the rest would cost a new
 * contract surface parsed once per row per page, and would only be coherent if
 * the other cast row kinds on this path were validated too.
 *
 * It also deliberately refuses rather than filtering. Dropping the offending
 * row would keep the page rendering while hiding a real customer order from the
 * operator handling it - a worse failure than a named refusal.
 */
export declare function assertOmsOrderRowCurrency(rows: ReadonlyArray<{
    id: string;
    currency: string;
}>, currency?: OmsCurrencyPolicy): void;
export declare class CommerceOmsConflictError extends Error {
    readonly details: Record<string, unknown>;
    constructor(message?: string, details?: Record<string, unknown>);
}
```

## dist/data/binding.d.ts

```ts
import { type PostgresDataGatewayEnv } from "./postgresGateway.js";
import type { DataGatewayPort } from "@openlup/core/platform-runtime";
import type { SupabaseCreateClient } from "./supabaseClientFactory.js";
type Env = Record<string, string | undefined>;
type ActorClaims = {
    sub?: string;
    role?: string;
} | null;
/** Actor-only data access for one request. */
export type ActorOnlyDataPort = {
    asActor: <T>(claims: ActorClaims, work: (gateway: unknown) => Promise<T>) => Promise<T>;
};
export interface DataPortBindingOptions {
    /** Replaces the bindings' supabase-js `createClient` for this call (tests). */
    createClientImpl?: SupabaseCreateClient;
    resolveAccessToken?: (claims: ActorClaims) => string | null;
}
export interface DataBindingDependencies {
    /** The supabase-js `createClient` used by every supabase gateway these bindings build. */
    createSupabaseClient: SupabaseCreateClient;
    /** Builds the direct-PostgreSQL gateway; defaults to `createPostgresDataGateway`. */
    createPostgresGateway?: (env: PostgresDataGatewayEnv) => DataGatewayPort;
}
export interface DataBindings {
    /** Resolve the DataGatewayPort for a bundle's declared data capability kind. */
    bindDataPort(dataKind: string, env: Env, requestOptions?: DataPortBindingOptions): DataGatewayPort | null;
    /** Bind one request to actor-only data access. */
    bindRequestActorDataPort(accessToken: string, env: Env, requestOptions?: DataPortBindingOptions): ActorOnlyDataPort | null;
    /** Resolve and bind the active bundle's data gateway, cached per bundle and data configuration. */
```

```ts
import type { Pool } from "pg";
import type { DataGatewayPort } from "@openlup/core/platform-runtime";
export interface PostgresDataGatewayEnv {
    /** Postgres connection string (DATABASE_URL). Empty until a node-postgres deploy supplies it. */

}

    /** Injection seam: tests pass a stub pool; production lazily builds a real pg.Pool from env. */

}

/** Keep PostgREST-compatible, lossless timestamp strings at the gateway edge. */
export declare function createPostgresGatewayTypeOverrides(TypeOverridesCtor: typeof import("pg").TypeOverrides): import("pg").TypeOverrides;
/**
 * Capability-local, role-free transaction lane.
 *
 * The platform manifest intentionally creates no service role. This is not an
 * alternate DataGatewayPort access mode: a deployment hands one lane to exactly
 * one adapter that calls its own rail RPCs, and names that lane after the
 * capability, because the name is the access rule. Its lifecycle reuses the same
 * pooled BEGIN/callback/COMMIT|ROLLBACK/release implementation as the public
 * gateway below, but it never changes the connection role.

}

 * method runs work (lazy, like the supabase adapter), so the slot binds even before DATABASE_URL is
 * present (keeps composeBundle({}) non-null). The pool is created once and reused across calls.
 */
export declare function createPostgresDataGateway(env: PostgresDataGatewayEnv, options?: PostgresDataGatewayOptions): DataGatewayPort;
/**
 * Build one role-free, per-operation lane. Do not use this for generic service
 * work: DataGatewayPort.asService remains the mandatory service_role boundary.
 * Only a named owner-authority capability of the deployment may hold a lane.
 */
export declare function createPostgresRoleFreeTransactionLane(env: PostgresDataGatewayEnv, options?: PostgresDataGatewayOptions): PostgresRoleFreeTransactionLane;
/** Resolve the gateway env from process env (DATABASE_URL), mirroring the supabase binding shape. */
```

```ts
/** PostgREST-shaped error the consumers narrow on (`.code`, `.message`, `.details`, `.hint`). */
export interface PgShimError {
    code?: string;
    message?: string;
    details?: string;
    hint?: string;
}
export interface PgShimResult<T = unknown> {
    data: T;
    error: PgShimError | null;
    count?: number | null;
}
/** Minimal query surface we need from a pg client (Pool acquires a PoolClient per request). */
export interface PgQueryExecutor {
    query(text: string, values?: unknown[]): Promise<{
        rows: Record<string, unknown>[];
    }>;
}
export type Filter = {
    kind: "eq" | "gte" | "lte" | "gt";
    column: string;
    value: unknown;
} | {
    kind: "in";
    column: string;
    values: unknown[];
} | {
    kind: "ilike";
    column: string;
    value: string;
} | {
    kind: "is";
    column: string;
    value: unknown;
};
export interface OrderBy {
    column: string;
    ascending: boolean;
}
export declare function quoteIdent(raw: string): string;
export declare function renderColumns(columns: string): string;
export declare function renderFilter(filter: Filter, params: ParamAccumulator): string;
/**
 * PostgREST `count=exact` rendered as a SEPARATE aggregate over the same FROM/WHERE — deliberately
 * NOT a `COUNT(*) OVER()` folded into the row query. PostgREST's exact count ignores limit/offset,
 * and a window function emits no row at all for an empty page, so an offset past the end would
 * report 0 instead of the true total. Keeping it separate also leaves the row projection
 * byte-identical: there is no synthetic column to strip before cardinality shaping.
 */
export declare function renderCountQuery(table: string, filters: readonly Filter[], params: ParamAccumulator): string;
/**
 * PostgREST refuses an out-of-bounds page instead of answering an empty one -- but ONLY when it
 * knows the total, i.e. when an exact count was requested; without one it answers 200 and `[]`,
 * which is what this shim already did. The boundary is strict: offset === total is a legal empty
 * page, offset === total + 1 is not. Measured against PostgREST 14.10 before this was written,
 * including the `details` sentence, which carries the two numbers a caller pages on.
 */
export declare function rangeNotSatisfiable(offset: number | null, total: number | null): PgShimError | null;
/**
 * INSERT ... VALUES ... RETURNING, with the column list taken from the FIRST row and every later
 * row required to carry the same key set. Deriving the columns from row 0 alone and binding the
 * rest positionally is how a batch used to lose a column silently: an extra key vanished, a missing
 * one bound NULL over a DEFAULT. PostgREST answers PGRST102 "All object keys must match" to exactly
 * this payload -- and keeps answering it under `Prefer: missing=default`, measured -- so the shim
 * refuses too. Refusal, not union: parity is the point, and this builder exposes no Prefer header
 * through which a caller could ask for anything else.
 */
export declare function renderInsertStatement(table: string, rows: readonly Record<string, unknown>[], projection: string, params: ParamAccumulator): string;
export declare function unwrapRpcRows(rows: Record<string, unknown>[], functionName: string): unknown;
/** Accumulates values and hands back $1,$2,… placeholders — the single injection-safety chokepoint. */
export declare class ParamAccumulator {
    readonly values: unknown[];
    add(value: unknown): string;
}
export declare function joinParts(parts: string[]): string;
export declare function toShimError(error: unknown): PgShimError;
```

```ts
import { type Server } from "node:http";
import type { HttpRuntimePort } from "@openlup/core/platform-runtime";

    load: EntrypointLoader;

 * entrypoint receives for this path, or null when the path is not its own. The body is buffered.
 */

}

    destination: string;
    permanent: boolean;
}
export interface NodeCsrFallback {

    routeFamilies: readonly string[];
}
export interface NodeHttpRuntimeOptions {
    /** The deployment's BFF route table. */

    /** The dispatcher that matches `routes` (see `createBffDispatcher`). */
    dispatch: BffDispatch;
    /** Validated public-route contract supplied by the host composition root. */
    routing?: {
        legacyRedirects: readonly NodeLegacyRedirect[];
        ssgPaths: readonly string[];
        csrFallback: NodeCsrFallback;
    };
    /** Deployment header policy, including the content security policy. */
    security: NodeSecurityHeaderPolicy;
    /** robots.txt body for a request host; without it `/robots.txt` is served like any other path. */
    robotsTxt?: (host: string | undefined) => string;

     * BFF router at `/api/bff-router`. A path without a handler is treated like any other path.
     */
    entrypoints?: {
        cspReport?: EntrypointLoader;
        bffRouter?: EntrypointLoader;

    extraEntrypoints?: readonly HttpEntrypointRegistration[];

    pathEntrypoints?: readonly HttpPathEntrypoint[];
    /** Deployment path-rule handlers checked after legacy redirects and before static documents. */
    fallbackPathEntrypoints?: readonly HttpPathEntrypoint[];
    /** Registry-derived allowlist; undeclared job ids must resolve null. */
    scheduledEntrypoint?: (jobId: string) => EntrypointLoader | null;
    /** Explicit host deadlines; missing cells use the bounded defaults below. */
    durations?: {
        bff?: number;
        cspReport?: number;
        bffRouter?: number;
        scheduled?: (jobId: string) => number;
    };
```

```ts
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import type { HttpResponse } from "./types.js";
/** Deployment-owned header policy applied to every response of the Node runtime. */
export interface NodeSecurityHeaderPolicy {

    /** Static-asset path prefixes that receive the long-lived asset Cache-Control. */
    readonly assetCachePrefixes: readonly string[];
    /** Path families (the path or anything below it) that receive `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`. */
    readonly noindexFamilies: readonly string[];
    /** Exact paths that receive `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`. */
    readonly noindexExacts: readonly string[];
    /** True for a production host; every other host receives the strict non-production X-Robots-Tag. */
    readonly isProductionHost: (host: string | undefined) => boolean;
}
/** Apply the deployment's global and path-specific headers for this route. */
export declare function applySecurityHeaders(res: ServerResponse, pathname: string, host: string | undefined, policy: NodeSecurityHeaderPolicy): void;
/** Pluggable static reader so tests can inject an in-memory dist/ without touching the filesystem. */
export interface StaticAssetReader {
    /** Read a built asset (returns null if it does not exist). */
    read(relativePath: string): Promise<{
        body: Buffer;

/** A path "looks like" a built asset if it has a file extension (so we 404 missing assets, not SPA). */

 * and the path looks like an asset, return 404. Otherwise, when
 * `allowRouteDocument` is true and its
 * lookup finds a route document, return that document with 200. If no route
 * document was returned, `allowFallback` true returns the SPA index with 200;
 * otherwise return 404. Both flags default to false. The host must enable these
 * options only for admitted routes; this helper does not identify application
 * routes.
 *
 * The middle step is not decoration. The build writes each pre-rendered route as
 * `<route>/index.html` and writes the HOME document to `index.html`; skipping that lookup hands
 * every admitted pre-rendered URL the home document instead of its own. The host
 * supplies the route-document and fallback decisions; this helper does not infer
 * application routes.
```

```ts
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
/** A Node request decorated with the parsed query, cookies and body. */
export interface HttpRequest extends IncomingMessage {
    query: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;
    cookies?: Record<string, string>;
    body?: unknown;
    /** Cooperative cancellation owned by the portable host adapter. */
    abortSignal?: AbortSignal;
}
/** A Node response decorated with the helper methods hosted functions provide. */
export interface HttpResponse extends ServerResponse {
    status(code: number): this;
    json(body: unknown): this;
    send(body: string | Buffer | object): this;
    redirect(url: string): this;
    redirect(status: number, url: string): this;
}
/** A default-export handler shared by every physical API entrypoint. */
export type HttpHandler = (req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse) => Promise<unknown> | unknown;
/** A lazily loaded module exposing one default-export handler. */
export type EntrypointLoader = () => Promise<{
    default: HttpHandler;
}>;
```

## dist/http/webhookSignature.d.ts

```ts
import type { HttpRequest } from "./types.js";
/** Maximum body size accepted by readRawBody, in bytes. */
export declare const MAX_WEBHOOK_BODY_BYTES = 1048576;
/** Thrown by readRawBody when its streamed or fallback body exceeds MAX_WEBHOOK_BODY_BYTES. */
export declare class WebhookBodyTooLargeError extends Error {
    constructor();
}
/**
 * Read an async-iterable request stream before consulting `req.body` and return
 * its chunks as concatenated UTF-8 text. Stream input is capped at
 * `MAX_WEBHOOK_BODY_BYTES`. Even a zero-byte chunk is a stream result. Only
 * when the stream emits no chunks, or no stream is available, fall back to a
 * string or Buffer in `req.body`, or JSON-serialize an object; fallback text is
 * capped by its UTF-8 byte length. Callers verifying signatures must preserve
 * original raw content, which may be exposed as a stream, string or Buffer; a
 * parsed object fallback cannot reconstruct the original wire bytes.
 */
export declare function readRawBody(req: HttpRequest): Promise<string>;
export declare function verifyHmacSignature(input: {
    rawBody: string;
    signatureHeader: string | undefined;
    secret: string;
}): boolean;
/**
 * Constant-time comparison of two shared-secret strings.
 *
 * Exported because it is not payment-specific: any route holding a shared token has to compare it
 * the same way, and a second hand-rolled copy of a security primitive is how one of them quietly
 * stops being constant-time. The length check comes first because `timingSafeEqual` THROWS on a
 * length mismatch rather than returning false, and the length of a shared token is not the secret.
 */
export declare function timingSafeStringEqual(left: string, right: string): boolean;
```

## dist/pricing/commercePricingPolicyErrors.d.ts

```ts
/** The server cannot assign a policy right now. Caller-independent: retrying an
 * identical request may succeed after server-side configuration or readiness changes. */
export declare class CommercePricingPolicyUnavailableError extends Error {
    readonly reason: string;
    constructor(reason: string);
}
/**
 * Every reason a caller-side refusal can carry. ⛔ Each one answers `400`, which
 * puts it outside every 5xx monitor, so each one owes a dedicated host monitor.
 * A host observability check can compare this tuple with its configured monitors;
 * the tuple itself does not install or verify those monitors. Adding a member
 * here without adding a monitor is the regression this tuple lets a host check
 * for: reclassifying `v2_capability_required` from `503` to `400` once silently
 * removed the only alert on a site-wide unpriceable-quote outage.
 */
export declare const PRICING_POLICY_REQUEST_REASONS: readonly ["v2_capability_required", "v2_visitor_id_required"];
export type PricingPolicyRequestReason = (typeof PRICING_POLICY_REQUEST_REASONS)[number];
/** The request itself lacks the context a v2 assignment needs. Caller-fixable,
 * and never evidence that pricing is down. The reason is typed off
 * PRICING_POLICY_REQUEST_REASONS so a host observability check can include it. */
export declare class CommercePricingPolicyRequestError extends Error {
    readonly reason: PricingPolicyRequestReason;
    constructor(reason: PricingPolicyRequestReason);
}
export type SafePricingPolicyReason = "v2_capability_required" | "v2_visitor_id_required" | "signing_secret_unavailable" | "v1_assignment_rejected" | "v2_assignment_config_invalid" | "v2_readiness_not_ready" | "other";
export interface PricingPolicyFailureMapping {
    code: "BAD_REQUEST" | "UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE";
    details: {
        reason: "pricing_policy_request_invalid" | "pricing_policy_unavailable";
        stage: "pricing_policy";
        policyReason: SafePricingPolicyReason;
    };
}
/**
 * Single owner of the status a pricing-policy refusal earns, shared by every
 * route that resolves one. A caller-attributable gap answers `400`; only a
 * server that genuinely cannot price answers `503`, so a `503` on these routes
 * stays a truthful outage signal.
 */
export declare function mapPricingPolicyFailure(error: unknown): PricingPolicyFailureMapping | null;
```

```ts
import type { ResolvedAcquisitionTerms } from "./contracts/acquisitionTerms.js";
import type { HttpRequest, HttpResponse } from "../http/index.js";

import type { z } from "zod";

import { COMMERCE_CONTRACT_VERSION } from "./contracts/index.js";

import type { PricingPolicySnapshot } from "./contracts/offerPolicy.js";
export interface CommerceQuoteBatchHandlerDeps<Request extends CreateQuoteRequest = CreateQuoteRequest, Response extends CreateQuoteResponse = CreateQuoteResponse> {
    responder: BffResponder;
    acquisition?: {
        terms(quote: Response["quote"]): ResolvedAcquisitionTerms | undefined;
    };
    createQuoteBatchRequestSchema: z.ZodType<{
        quotes: Request[];
    }>;
    publicCreateQuoteBatchResponseSchema: z.ZodType<{
        contractVersion: typeof COMMERCE_CONTRACT_VERSION;

        source: "anonymous" | "resolved_client";

}
/**
 * For each validated quote entry, resolves optional eligibility and policy and
 * then calls the injected quote port; entries run concurrently. It returns each
 * result's quote in request order, and a failed call fails the whole batch. This
 * handler supplies no shared timestamp or read context; any cross-entry sharing
 * belongs to the injected port. It returns quote bodies only and does not apply
 * the separate single-route acceptance or fallback steps.
 */
export declare function createCommerceQuoteBatchHandler<Request extends CreateQuoteRequest, Response extends CreateQuoteResponse>({ responder, createQuoteBatchRequestSchema, publicCreateQuoteBatchResponseSchema, quotePort, acquisition, resolveCustomerEligibility, resolvePricingPolicy, }: CommerceQuoteBatchHandlerDeps<Request, Response>): (req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse) => Promise<void>;
```

```ts
import type { CommerceCurrency, CommerceMoney, CommerceTaxBreakdown } from "./money.js";
import type { CommerceCodeRejectionDetail } from "./evidence.js";
export declare const COMMERCE_CONTRACT_VERSION = "commerce.v0";
export interface CommerceQuotePricingComponent {
    scope: "line" | "order";
    componentType: "base_unit" | "mode_discount" | "qty_tier" | "promo" | "loyalty" | "shipping" | "bundle";
    amountMinor: number;
    reasonCode: string;
    reasonPayload: Record<string, unknown>;
}
/** Hosts may extend this context with their own validated policy. */
export interface CommerceQuoteContext {
    mode: "one_time" | "subscription";
    cadenceDays?: number | null;
    promoCodes: string[];
}
export interface CommerceQuoteLine {
    sku: string;
    productSlug: string;
    quantity: number;
    unitPriceGross: CommerceMoney;
    lineSubtotalGross: CommerceMoney;
    tax: CommerceTaxBreakdown;
    pricingComponents?: CommerceQuotePricingComponent[];
}
export type CommercePromotionCustomerSemantic = "first_purchase_10" | "first_subscription_50" | "bundle_5";
export interface CommerceQuoteDiscount {
    promotionId: string;
    code?: string | null;
    label?: string;
    appliesTo: "order_total" | "line" | "shipping";
    amountOffMinor: number;
    reasonCode: string;
    /** Stable presentation identity for canonical offers; never inferred from an editable name. */
    customerSemantic?: CommercePromotionCustomerSemantic;
    promotionEngineVersion?: "promotion-engine.v2";
    promotionCodeId?: string;
    promotionCodeRevision?: number;
    promotionDefinitionFingerprint?: string;
    promotionCodeScopes?: Array<"one_time" | "subscription_initial">;
    promotionMinimumReferenceMinor?: number;
    promotionCodeValidTo?: string | null;
    promotionBenefitKind?: "target_percentage" | "percentage" | "fixed_amount" | "free_shipping";
    promotionBenefitValueBps?: number;
    promotionBenefitValueMinor?: number;
    floorApplied?: boolean;
}
/**
 * Why a customer-submitted promo code did not produce a discount, surfaced so the FE
 * can show a specific message instead of a generic "invalid". `better_price_exists` is
 * the one non-error reason (eligible but unused because an automatic offer is equally
 * good or better; the FE renders it neutrally); `scope_not_applicable` means the code
 * is real but does not apply to this order type. The last two are v2-engine-only reasons.
 */
export type CommerceCodeRejectionReason = "not_recognized" | "already_used" | "not_eligible" | "expired" | "better_price_exists" | "scope_not_applicable";
export interface CommerceCodeRejection {
    code: string;
    reason: CommerceCodeRejectionReason;
}
export interface CommerceQuote {
    currency: CommerceCurrency;
    taxIncluded: true;
    lines: CommerceQuoteLine[];
    discounts: CommerceQuoteDiscount[];
    pricingComponents?: CommerceQuotePricingComponent[];
    context?: CommerceQuoteContext;
    /** Submitted coupon codes that produced no discount, with the reason. Omitted when empty. */
    codeRejections?: CommerceCodeRejection[];
    /** UI-safe specifics for selected rejected codes. Omitted when no detail is available. */
    codeRejectionDetails?: CommerceCodeRejectionDetail[];
    subtotalGross: CommerceMoney;
    discountTotalGross: CommerceMoney;
    /** Gross shipping before any free-shipping promo. Optional; absent ⇒ shipping not priced (legacy/0). */
    shippingGross?: CommerceMoney;
    /** Gross shipping discount from a free-shipping promo (≤ shippingGross). Optional; absent ⇒ 0. */
    shippingDiscountGross?: CommerceMoney;
    totalGross: CommerceMoney;
    netTotal: CommerceMoney;
    taxTotal: CommerceMoney;
}
export interface CreateQuoteRequest {
    mode: "one_time" | "subscription";
    lines: import("./money.js").CartLineInput[];
    cadenceDays?: number | null;
    promoCodes: string[];
}
export interface CreateQuoteResponse {
    contractVersion: typeof COMMERCE_CONTRACT_VERSION;
    quote: Omit<CommerceQuote, "lines"> & {
        lines: Array<CommerceQuoteLine & {
            catalogFacts?: import("./provenance.js").CatalogFactsProvenance;
        }>;
    };
}
```

```ts
import type { CreateQuoteRequest } from "./contracts/quote.js";

    listQuoteCatalogItems(): Promise<Item[]>;

export declare function eligibleQtyForMode(request: CreateQuoteRequest, mode: Exclude<PricingMode, "any">): number;
export declare function resolveOneTimeComparison(input: {
    pricingResolverPort: PricingResolverPort;
    variantId: string;
    lineQty: number;
    eligibleCartQty: number;

}

 * derived by a validated policy or taken from the authority's explicit legacy
 * subscription observation; this layer never calls the generic resolver.
 */
export declare function resolveStrictQuotePrice(input: {
    commercePriceAuthorityPort: CommercePriceAuthorityPort;
    variantId: string;

    regionCode: string;
    currency: string;
    channel: string;
    atTime: string;

export declare function assertSubscriptionCheapest(input: {
    requestSku: string;
    modeAtLine: Exclude<PricingMode, "any">;
    resolved: ResolvedPrice;
    oneTime: ResolvedPrice | null;
    vatRateBps: number;
}): void;
export declare function toGrossMinor(price: ResolvedPrice, vatRateBps: number): number;
export declare function calculateIncludedVat(grossMinor: number, vatRateBps: number): {
    netMinor: number;
    vatMinor: number;
};
/**
 * Map one kernel pricing component onto the quote transport shape.
 *
 * A component type outside the transport union stops the quote loudly. It is never
 * cast through and never silently dropped: a dropped component breaks the breakdown
 * invariant (components must sum to the line total), so a quote would go out priced
 * from an incomplete explanation of its own total.
 */
export declare function mapPricingComponent(component: PricingComponent, scope: "line" | "order"): CommerceQuotePricingComponent;
/**
 * A money builder bound to one currency.
 *
 * This used to be a plain `money(amountMinor)` that stamped the settlement
 * currency as a literal, which meant the quote's declared `currency` and the
 * currency on every amount inside it came from two different places. They
 * agreed only because the port's `currency` option was typed as that same
 * literal. Binding the builder once per port makes the agreement structural:
 * there is no second place left for the two to disagree.
 */
export declare function moneyIn(currency: string): (amountMinor: number) => CommerceMoney;
export declare function sum(values: number[]): number;
```

```ts
        quote: Omit<CreateQuoteResponse["quote"], "context"> & {
            context: Context & {
                pricingPolicy?: PricingPolicySnapshot;
                shippingTerms?: ShippingTerms;
                acquisitionTerms?: ResolvedAcquisitionTerms;
            };
        };
    }>;
    createServerAuthoritativeQuote: (request: Request, options?: CreateQuoteOptions) => Promise<Omit<CreateQuoteResponse, "quote"> & {
        quote: Omit<CreateQuoteResponse["quote"], "context"> & {
            context: Context & {
                pricingPolicy?: PricingPolicySnapshot;
                shippingTerms?: ShippingTerms;
                acquisitionTerms?: ResolvedAcquisitionTerms;
            };
        };
    }>;
};
```

## dist/pricing/quotePorts.d.ts

```ts
import type { CreateQuoteRequest, CreateQuoteResponse } from "./contracts/quote.js";
import type { PricingPolicySnapshot } from "./contracts/offerPolicy.js";
/**
 * Server-resolved options for promo evaluation. Passed OUT-OF-BAND by the caller
 * (orchestration -> provisioned.clientId; preview -> session/null) — NEVER sourced
 * from the client request body — so `first_purchase` eligibility cannot be forged.
 */
export interface CreateQuoteOptions {
    clientId?: string | null;
    pricingPolicy?: PricingPolicySnapshot;
}
export interface CommerceQuotePort<Request = CreateQuoteRequest, Response = CreateQuoteResponse> {
    createQuote(request: Request, options?: CreateQuoteOptions): Promise<Response>;
    createServerAuthoritativeQuote?(request: Request, options?: CreateQuoteOptions): Promise<Response>;
}
export interface CommerceQuoteSnapshotVerifierPort<Response = CreateQuoteResponse> {
    verifyQuoteSnapshot(snapshot: Response): Promise<Response | void>;
}
export type CommerceQuoteSnapshotErrorCode = "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT_MISMATCH";
export declare class CommerceQuoteSnapshotError extends Error {
    readonly code: CommerceQuoteSnapshotErrorCode;
    readonly details: Record<string, unknown>;
    constructor(code: CommerceQuoteSnapshotErrorCode, message: string, details?: Record<string, unknown>);
}
export type CommerceQuoteErrorCode = "UNKNOWN_SKU" | "PRICE_NOT_CONFIGURED" | "UNSUPPORTED_TAX_PROFILE" | "PRICING_INVARIANT_VIOLATION";
export declare class CommerceQuoteError extends Error {
    readonly code: CommerceQuoteErrorCode;
    readonly details: Record<string, unknown>;
    constructor(code: CommerceQuoteErrorCode, message: string, details?: Record<string, unknown>);
}
```

```ts
import type { ShippingTerms } from "../shipping/index.js";
import type { ResolvedAcquisitionTerms } from "./contracts/acquisitionTerms.js";
import type { CreateQuoteRequest } from "./contracts/quote.js";
import type { CreateQuoteOptions } from "./quotePorts.js";
import type { QuotePromotionResult } from "./dbBackedCommerceQuotePort.js";
export interface QuoteShippingPromotionInput<Request extends CreateQuoteRequest> {
    request: Request;
    options?: CreateQuoteOptions;
    regionCode: string;
    currency: string;
    referenceProductMinor: number;
    subtotalGrossMinor: number;
    atTime: string;
}
export interface QuoteShippingPromotionResult extends QuotePromotionResult {
    shippingGrossMinor: number;
    hasShipping: boolean;
    shippingTerms?: ShippingTerms;
    acquisitionTerms?: ResolvedAcquisitionTerms;
}
```

```ts
import { z } from "./validation.js";

}, z.core.$strict>;
export declare const subscriptionPricePolicyRevisionSchema: z.ZodObject<{
    discountBps: z.ZodNumber;
    roundingQuantumMinor: z.ZodNumber;
    roundingRule: z.ZodEnum<{
        FLOOR_TO_QUANTUM: "FLOOR_TO_QUANTUM";
    }>;

    revisionNo: z.ZodNumber;
    priceListId: z.ZodGUID;
    regionCode: z.ZodString;
    currency: z.ZodString;
    channel: z.ZodString;
    effectiveFrom: z.ZodString;
    effectiveTo: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
    digest: z.ZodString;
}, z.core.$strict>;
export declare class SubscriptionPricePolicyDigestError extends Error {
    readonly code: SubscriptionPricePolicyDigestRefusal;
    constructor(code: SubscriptionPricePolicyDigestRefusal);
}
/** The exact tuple whose canonical digest is persisted with a policy revision. */

/** Hashes application-owned canonical UTF-8 bytes; never a database JSON rendering. */

/** Parses a persisted revision and refuses a digest outside its canonical UTF-8 tuple. */
```

## dist/shipping/addressSchemas.d.ts

```ts
import type { AddressCanonPostalCodeLookupRequest, AddressCanonPostalCodeLookupResponse, AddressCanonLocalitiesLookupRequest, AddressCanonLocalitiesLookupResponse, AddressCanonStreetsLookupRequest, AddressCanonStreetsLookupResponse, CustomerAddressesRequest, CustomerAddressesResponse, CustomerAddressUpsertRequest, CustomerAddressDeleteRequest } from "./contracts/address.js";
type Schema<T> = {
    safeParse(input: unknown): {
        success: true;
        data: T;
    } | {
        success: false;
        error: {
            flatten(): unknown;
        };
    };
};
/** Host-owned format, source and geographic validation. */
export interface AddressLookupSchemas {
    addressCanonPostalCodeLookupRequestSchema: Schema<AddressCanonPostalCodeLookupRequest>;
    addressCanonPostalCodeLookupResponseSchema: Schema<AddressCanonPostalCodeLookupResponse>;
    addressCanonLocalitiesLookupRequestSchema: Schema<AddressCanonLocalitiesLookupRequest>;
    addressCanonLocalitiesLookupResponseSchema: Schema<AddressCanonLocalitiesLookupResponse>;
    addressCanonStreetsLookupRequestSchema: Schema<AddressCanonStreetsLookupRequest>;
    addressCanonStreetsLookupResponseSchema: Schema<AddressCanonStreetsLookupResponse>;
}
/** Host-owned validation and mutation-conflict identity. */
export interface CustomerAddressSchemas {
    customerAddressesRequestSchema: Schema<CustomerAddressesRequest>;
    customerAddressesResponseSchema: Schema<CustomerAddressesResponse>;
    customerAddressUpsertRequestSchema: Schema<CustomerAddressUpsertRequest>;
    customerAddressDeleteRequestSchema: Schema<CustomerAddressDeleteRequest>;
    isMutationConflict(error: unknown): error is Error;
}
export {};
```

```ts
}
```

```ts
export type DeliveryKind = "parcel-locker" | "courier";
export type DeliveryProviderKind = string;
export type DeliveryCarrierKind = string;
export type DeliveryService = string;
export interface PickupPoint {
    id: string;
    provider: DeliveryCarrierKind;
    name: string;
    address: {
        line1: string;
        postalCode: string;
        city: string;
        country: string;
    };
}
export interface DeliveryOption {
    id: string;
    kind: DeliveryKind;
    deliveryKind: DeliveryKind;
    providerKind: DeliveryProviderKind;
    carrierKind: DeliveryCarrierKind;
    carrierCode: string;
    service: DeliveryService;
    serviceCode: string;
    label: string;
    description: string;
    pickupPointRequired: boolean;
    addressRequired: boolean;
}
export interface DeliveryOptionsResponse {
    contractVersion: "shipping.delivery_selection.v1";
    options: DeliveryOption[];
}
export interface PickupPointValidationRequest {
    pointId: string;
    carrierKind: DeliveryCarrierKind;
}
export interface PickupPointValidationResponse {
    contractVersion: "shipping.delivery_selection.v1";
    valid: boolean;
    pickupPoint: PickupPoint | null;
}
export interface PickupPointSearchRequest {
    carrierKind: DeliveryCarrierKind;
    latitude?: number;
    longitude?: number;
    postalCode?: string;
    city?: string;
    query?: string;
    limit?: number;
}
export interface PickupPointSearchResult {
    id: string;
    carrierKind: DeliveryCarrierKind;
    name: string;
    address: PickupPoint["address"];
    location: {
        latitude: number;
        longitude: number;
    } | null;
    locationDescription: string | null;
    openingHours: string | null;
    distanceMeters: number | null;
    pointKind: "locker" | "service_point" | null;
    appAssisted: boolean;
}
export interface PickupPointSearchResponse {
    contractVersion: "shipping.pickup_point_search.v1";
    carrierKind: DeliveryCarrierKind;
    points: PickupPointSearchResult[];
}
export type DeliverySelectionSummaryKind = "courier" | "parcel-locker";
export interface DeliverySelectionSummary {
    deliveryKind: DeliverySelectionSummaryKind;
    providerKind: string | null;
    carrierKind: string | null;
    carrierCode: string | null;
    serviceCode: string | null;
    pickupPoint: {
        id: string;
        name: string;
        address: {
            line1: string;
            postalCode: string;
            city: string;
            country: string;
        } | null;
    } | null;
    source?: string | null;
}
export type DeliverySelectionSourceKey = "shippingDeliveryContact" | "orderDeliveryContactOverride" | "orderDeliveryContact" | "fulfillmentMetadata" | "orderMetadata" | "orderRuntimeFinalize" | "shippingAddressSnapshot" | "addressMetadata";
export interface DeliverySelectionSources {
    /** commerce_fulfillment_orders.metadata.selectedDelivery */
    fulfillmentMetadata?: unknown;
    /** commerce_orders.metadata.selectedDelivery (and .runtimeFinalize.selectedDelivery) */
    orderMetadata?: unknown;
    /** shipping_address_snapshot.selectedDelivery */
    shippingAddressSnapshot?: unknown;
    /** addresses.metadata.selectedDelivery */
    addressMetadata?: unknown;
}
export interface DeliverySelectionEvidence {
    /** The canonical selectedDelivery object, or null when no source carries one. */
    selection: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    /** Normalized (trimmed, lowercased) providerKind, or null when absent/blank. */
    providerKind: string | null;
    /** Which authoritative source was resolved (priority order), or null. A
     * canonical contact can be the source even when its selection is invalid. */
    source: DeliverySelectionSourceKey | null;
}
```

```ts
        userId: string;
    } | null>;
}
type Schema<T> = {
    safeParse(input: unknown): {
        success: true;
        data: T;
    } | {
        success: false;
        error: {
            flatten(): unknown;
        };
    };
};

    adminSetShippingRateRequestSchema: Schema<AdminSetShippingRateRequest>;
    adminSetShippingRateResponseSchema: Schema<AdminSetShippingRateResponse>;
}
export {};
```
